# About this item

*    Fits Filters from 2 1/2" to 3 1/8". 
*    Removes all oil filters from 2 1/2" to 3 1/8" in diameter. 
*    This extra large range enables the 63600 wrench to fit most all import car filters plus the common 3" domestic car filters. 
*    Steel jaws grip filter without slipping. Works in tight spots from the front of the filter using 3/8" drive. 
*    This is to be used for removing the filter, not installing.

### Lisle 63600 Oil Filter Tool

Removes all oil filters from 2 1/2" to 3 1/8" in diameter. This extra large range enables the 63600 wrench to fit almost all import car filters plus the common 3" domestic car filters. Steel jaws grip filter without slipping. Works in tight spots from the front of the filter using 3/8" drive.

## Product Description

Fits Filters from 2 1/2" to 3 1/8".  
  
Removes all oil filters from 2 1/2" to 3 1/8" in diameter. This extra large range enables the 63600 wrench to fit most all import car filters plus the common 3" domestic car filters. Steel jaws grip filter without slipping. Works in tight spots from the front of the filter using 3/8" drive. This is to be used for removing the filter, not installing.

[How customer reviews and ratings work](javascript:void(0))

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

[Learn more how customers reviews work on Amazon](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_omni_lm_btn?nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)

### Customers say

Customers find the oil filter wrench easy to use, particularly for changing oil, and consider it a great investment for basic maintenance. The tool is durable, with one customer noting it's strong enough to turn stuck filters, and they appreciate its versatility as a welcome addition to their tool kit. The fit and grip receive mixed feedback - while some say it fits multiple sizes and grips amazingly, others report it won't fit smaller filters and doesn't grip at all. Customers disagree on whether the wrench can tighten filters.

_5 out of 5 stars_

##### Unbelievably wonderful

Wow! Unbelievable! I used to fight with my motorhomes oil filter all of the time. On the f53 engine Ford puts the filter in a very tight area. I figured I would give this wrench a shot and see what happens. The first thing I did was I looked at the large filter for the motorhome and measured the outside and then looked at product description of this wrench to ensure that it would fit. It said it would fit so I bought it. I just did an oil change on my motorhome and used this wrench. It fit perfectly. I was able to get a ratchet on it. And the oil filter came off like nothing. And then did an oil change on my subaru. The filter is very easy to get at. But the shop that did the last oil change put the filter on way too tight and to get it off I had to use this filter wrench a second time. Unbelievable I can't believe I did not buy this oil filter wrench sooner. If you have ever fought with an oil filter get this wrench.

## About this Item

*   Fits Filters from 2 1/2" to 3 1/8".
*   Removes all oil filters from 2 1/2" to 3 1/8" in diameter.
*   This extra large range enables the 63600 wrench to fit most all import car filters plus the common 3" domestic car filters.
*   Steel jaws grip filter without slipping. Works in tight spots from the front of the filter using 3/8" drive.
*   This is to be used for removing the filter, not installing.

## Product Description

 Fits Filters from 2 1/2" to 3 1/8". Removes all oil filters from 2 1/2" to 3 1/8" in diameter. This extra large range enables the 63600 wrench to fit most all import car filters plus the common 3" domestic car filters. Steel jaws grip filter without slipping. Works in tight spots from the front of the filter using 3/8" drive. This is to be used for removing the filter, not installing.
